      success: ( data ) => {
        if ( data.agents && data.agents.length && window.innerWidth >= 1024 ) {

        }
      }
    } );
  }

  interval = setInterval( () => {
    const lastOpen = window.sessionStorage.getItem( 'hs-proactive-beacon-time' );
    if ( !lastOpen || Date.now() - lastOpen > 10 * 60 * 1000 ) {
      open();
    }
  }, 10000 );
